About This Report

Report Description

Passive ZAP requestor report for the public TwinaForms published form page. This report does not run an active attack scan.

Report Parameters

Contexts

The following contexts were selected to be included:

  • TwinaForms Published Form Page

Sites

The following sites were included:

  • https://forms.twinaforms.com

(If no sites were selected, all sites were included by default.)

An included site must also be within one of the included contexts for its data to be included in the report.

Risk levels

Included: High, Medium, Low, Informational

Excluded: None

Confidence levels

Included: User Confirmed, High, Medium, Low, False Positive

Excluded: None

Alerts

  1. Risk=Medium, Confidence=High (1)

    1. https://forms.twinaforms.com (1)

      1. Content Security Policy (CSP) Header Not Set (1)
        1. GET https://forms.twinaforms.com/twinaforms/form1?email=liam.carter@example.com
          Alert tags
          • CWE-693
          • OWASP_2021_A05
          • OWASP_2017_A06
          • POLICY_QA_STD =
          • POLICY_PENTEST =
          • SYSTEMIC
          Alert description

          Content Security Policy (CSP) is an added layer of security that helps to detect and mitigate certain types of attacks, including Cross Site Scripting (XSS) and data injection attacks. These attacks are used for everything from data theft to site defacement or distribution of malware. CSP provides a set of standard HTTP headers that allow website owners to declare approved sources of content that browsers should be allowed to load on that page — covered types are JavaScript, CSS, HTML frames, fonts, images and embeddable objects such as Java applets, ActiveX, audio and video files.

window.grecaptcha.reset==="function"){try{window.grecaptcha.reset();}catch(e){}}}function dateFormatHint(format){return format==="eu"?"DD/MM/YYYY":"MM/DD/YYYY";}function isValidDateParts(year,month,day){const test=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));return test.getUTCFullYear()===year&&test.getUTCMonth()+1===month&&test.getUTCDate()===day;}function parseDateValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);if(match){const year=Number(match[1]);const month=Number(match[2]);const day=Number(match[3]);if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:match[1]+"-"+match[2]+"-"+match[3]};}const normalized=raw.replace(/[.\-]/g,"/");match=normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);if(!match){return null;}const first=Number(match[1]);const second=Number(match[2]);const year=Number(match[3]);const month=format==="eu"?second:first;const day=format==="eu"?first:second;if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}const monthText=String(month).padStart(2,"0");const dayText=String(day).padStart(2,"0");return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:String(year).padStart(4,"0")+"-"+monthText+"-"+dayText};}function formatParsedDate(parsed,format){if(!parsed){return "";}const day=String(parsed.day).padStart(2,"0");const month=String(parsed.month).padStart(2,"0");const year=String(parsed.year).padStart(4,"0");return format==="eu"?day+"/"+month+"/"+year:month+"/"+day+"/"+year;}function formatDateForField(value,field){const format=field&&field.dataset&&field.dataset.dateFormat?field.dataset.dateFormat:"us";const validateDateField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsDate!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.dateFormat||"us";const raw=String(el.value||"").trim();if(!raw){el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}const parsed=parseDateValue(raw,format);if(!parsed){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateInvalid","Please enter a valid date in {format} format."),{format:dateFormatHint(format)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}const minDate=el.dataset.effectiveMinDate||"";const maxDate=el.dataset.effectiveMaxDate||"";if(minDate&&parsed.iso<minDate){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateMin","Please enter a date on or after {date}."),{date:formatDateForField(minDate,el)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}function timeFormatHint(format){return format==="12h"?"h:mm AM/PM":"HH:mm";}function parseTimeValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match;if(format==="12h"){match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*([AaPp][Mm])$/);if(!match){return null;}let hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<1||hours>12||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}const meridiem=match[3].toUpperCase();if(meridiem==="AM"&&hours===12){hours=0;}else if(meridiem==="PM"&&hours<12){hours+=12;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match){return null;}const hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<0||hours>23||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}function formatTimeForField(value,field){const value==null?"":String(value);}if(format!=="12h"){return parsed;}const parts=parsed.split(":");const hour24=Number(parts[0]);const meridiem=hour24>=12?"PM":"AM";const hour12=hour24%12||12;return String(hour12)+":"+parts[1]+" "+meridiem;}function validateTimeField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsTime!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.timeFormat||"24h";const readFieldValue(fieldKey){const fields=form.querySelectorAll("[name='"+fieldKey+"']");if(!fields.length){return "";}const first=fields[0];if(first.type==="checkbox"){return !!first.checked;}if(first.type==="radio"){const checked=form.querySelector("[name='"+fieldKey+"']:checked");return checked?checked.value:"";}return first.value||"";}function evaluateSingleCondition(condition){if(!condition||!condition.fieldKey||!condition.operator){return true;}const actual=readFieldValue(condition.fieldKey);const compare=condition.value||"";if(condition.operator==="equals"){return String(actual)===compare;}if(condition.operator==="notEquals"){return String(actual)!==compare;}if(condition.operator==="isTrue"){return actual===true||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="true";}if(condition.operator==="isFalse"){return actual===false||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="false"||String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isBlank"){return String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isNotBlank"){return String(actual)!=="";}return true;}function evaluateConditionExpression(results,expression){if(!results.length){return true;}if(results.length===1){return results[0];}const normalized=((expression||"").trim()||results.map(function(_,index){return String(index+1);}).join(" AND ")).replace(/\(/g," ( ").replace(/\)/g," ) ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();const tokens=normalized?normalized.split(" "):[];const values=[];const operators=[];function applyOperator(){if(values.length<2||!operators.length){return;}const operator=operators.pop();const right=values.pop();const left=values.pop();values.push(operator==="AND"?(left&&right):(left||right));}function precedence(operator){return operator==="AND"?2:1;}for(let index=0;index<tokens.length;index+=1){const token=tokens[index];if(!token){continue;}if(token==="("){operators.push(token);continue;}if(token===")"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("){applyOperator();}if(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]==="("){operators.pop();}continue;}if(token==="AND"||token==="OR"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("&&precedence(operators[operators.length-1])>=precedence(token)){applyOperator();}operators.push(token);continue;}if(/^\d+$/.test(token)){const refIndex=Number(token)-1;values.push(refIndex>=0&&refIndex<results.length?results[refIndex]:false);}}while(operators.length){applyOperator();}return values.length?!!values[0]:false;}function evaluateElementConditions(el){let conditions=[];try{conditions=JSON.parse(el.dataset.conditions||"[]")||[];}catch(error){conditions=[];}if(!conditions.length&&el.dataset.conditionSource){conditions=[{fieldKey:el.dataset.conditionSource,operator:el.dataset.conditionOperator,value:el.dataset.conditionValue||""}];}if(!conditions.length){return true;}const results=conditions.map(function(condition){return evaluateSingleCondition(condition);});return evaluateConditionExpression(results,el.dataset.conditionExpression||"");}function setFieldValue(fieldKey,value){if(formulaFieldKeySet().has(String(fieldKey||""))){return;}const if(el.type==="radio"){el.checked=String(el.value)===String(value);}else if(el.dataset.nativeformsDate==="true"){el.value=formatDateForField(value,el);validateDateField(el,false);}else if(el.dataset.nativeformsTime==="true"){el.value=formatTimeForField(value,el);validateTimeField(el,false);}else{el.value=value==null?"":value;}});}function readAllFieldValues(){const values={};form.querySelectorAll("[name]").forEach(function(control){const repeatDeleteState={};let lastPrefillPayload=null;function readRepeatFieldValueByKey(rowEl,fieldKey){const           Solution

          Ensure that your web server, application server, load balancer, etc. is configured to set the Content-Security-Policy header.

  2. Risk=Low, Confidence=High (1)

    1. https://forms.twinaforms.com (1)

      1. Server Leaks Version Information via "Server" HTTP Response Header Field (1)
        1. GET https://forms.twinaforms.com/twinaforms/form1?email=liam.carter@example.com

          Alert description

          The web/application server is leaking version information via the "Server" HTTP response header. Access to such information may facilitate attackers identifying other vulnerabilities your web/application server is subject to.

getCaptchaToken(){const responseField=form.querySelector("textarea[name='g-recaptcha-response'],input[name='g-recaptcha-response']");return responseField?responseField.value||"":"";}function resetCaptcha(){const responseField=form.querySelector("textarea[name='g-recaptcha-response'],input[name='g-recaptcha-response']");if(responseField){responseField.value="";}if(window.grecaptcha&&typeof window.grecaptcha.reset==="function"){try{window.grecaptcha.reset();}catch(e){}}}function dateFormatHint(format){return format==="eu"?"DD/MM/YYYY":"MM/DD/YYYY";}function isValidDateParts(year,month,day){const test=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));return test.getUTCFullYear()===year&&test.getUTCMonth()+1===month&&test.getUTCDate()===day;}function parseDateValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);if(match){const year=Number(match[1]);const month=Number(match[2]);const day=Number(match[3]);if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:match[1]+"-"+match[2]+"-"+match[3]};}const normalized=raw.replace(/[.\-]/g,"/");match=normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);if(!match){return null;}const first=Number(match[1]);const second=Number(match[2]);const year=Number(match[3]);const month=format==="eu"?second:first;const day=format==="eu"?first:second;if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}const monthText=String(month).padStart(2,"0");const dayText=String(day).padStart(2,"0");return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:String(year).padStart(4,"0")+"-"+monthText+"-"+dayText};}function formatParsedDate(parsed,format){if(!parsed){return "";}const day=String(parsed.day).padStart(2,"0");const month=String(parsed.month).padStart(2,"0");const year=String(parsed.year).padStart(4,"0");return format==="eu"?day+"/"+month+"/"+year:month+"/"+day+"/"+year;}function formatDateForField(value,field){const format=field&&field.dataset&&field.dataset.dateFormat?field.dataset.dateFormat:"us";const parsed=parseDateValue(value,format)||parseDateValue(value,"us")||parseDateValue(value,"eu");return parsed?formatParsedDate(parsed,format):value==null?"":String(value);}function validateDateField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsDate!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.dateFormat||"us";const raw=String(el.value||"").trim();if(!raw){el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}const parsed=parseDateValue(raw,format);if(!parsed){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateInvalid","Please enter a valid date in {format} format."),{format:dateFormatHint(format)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}const minDate=el.dataset.effectiveMinDate||"";const maxDate=el.dataset.effectiveMaxDate||"";if(minDate&&parsed.iso<minDate){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateMin","Please enter a date on or after {date}."),{date:formatDateForField(minDate,el)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}if(maxDate&&parsed.iso>maxDate){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateMax","Please enter a date on or before {date}."),{date:formatDateForField(maxDate,el)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}function timeFormatHint(format){return format==="12h"?"h:mm AM/PM":"HH:mm";}function parseTimeValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match;if(format==="12h"){match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*([AaPp][Mm])$/);if(!match){return null;}let hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<1||hours>12||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}const meridiem=match[3].toUpperCase();if(meridiem==="AM"&&hours===12){hours=0;}else if(meridiem==="PM"&&hours<12){hours+=12;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match){return null;}const hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<0||hours>23||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}function formatTimeForField(value,field){const format=field&&field.dataset&&field.dataset.timeFormat?field.dataset.timeFormat:"24h";const parsed=parseTimeValue(value,"24h")||parseTimeValue(value,"12h");if(!parsed){return value==null?"":String(value);}if(format!=="12h"){return parsed;}const parts=parsed.split(":");const hour24=Number(parts[0]);const meridiem=hour24>=12?"PM":"AM";const hour12=hour24%12||12;return String(hour12)+":"+parts[1]+" "+meridiem;}function validateTimeField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsTime!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.timeFormat||"24h";const readFieldValue(fieldKey){const fields=form.querySelectorAll("[name='"+fieldKey+"']");if(!fields.length){return "";}const first=fields[0];if(first.type==="checkbox"){return !!first.checked;}if(first.type==="radio"){const checked=form.querySelector("[name='"+fieldKey+"']:checked");return checked?checked.value:"";}return first.value||"";}function evaluateSingleCondition(condition){if(!condition||!condition.fieldKey||!condition.operator){return true;}const actual=readFieldValue(condition.fieldKey);const compare=condition.value||"";if(condition.operator==="equals"){return String(actual)===compare;}if(condition.operator==="notEquals"){return String(actual)!==compare;}if(condition.operator==="isTrue"){return actual===true||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="true";}if(condition.operator==="isFalse"){return actual===false||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="false"||String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isBlank"){return String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isNotBlank"){return String(actual)!=="";}return true;}function evaluateConditionExpression(results,expression){if(!results.length){return true;}if(results.length===1){return results[0];}const normalized=((expression||"").trim()||results.map(function(_,index){return String(index+1);}).join(" AND ")).replace(/\(/g," ( ").replace(/\)/g," ) ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();const tokens=normalized?normalized.split(" "):[];const values=[];const operators=[];function applyOperator(){if(values.length<2||!operators.length){return;}const operator=operators.pop();const right=values.pop();const left=values.pop();values.push(operator==="AND"?(left&&right):(left||right));}function precedence(operator){return operator==="AND"?2:1;}for(let index=0;index<tokens.length;index+=1){const token=tokens[index];if(!token){continue;}if(token==="("){operators.push(token);continue;}if(token===")"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("){applyOperator();}if(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]==="("){operators.pop();}continue;}if(token==="AND"||token==="OR"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("&&precedence(operators[operators.length-1])>=precedence(token)){applyOperator();}operators.push(token);continue;}if(/^\d+$/.test(token)){const refIndex=Number(token)-1;values.push(refIndex>=0&&refIndex<results.length?results[refIndex]:false);}}while(operators.length){applyOperator();}return values.length?!!values[0]:false;}function evaluateElementConditions(el){let conditions=[];try{conditions=JSON.parse(el.dataset.conditions||"[]")||[];}catch(error){conditions=[];}if(!conditions.length&&el.dataset.conditionSource){conditions=[{fieldKey:el.dataset.conditionSource,operator:el.dataset.conditionOperator,value:el.dataset.conditionValue||""}];}if(!conditions.length){return true;}const results=conditions.map(function(condition){return evaluateSingleCondition(condition);});return evaluateConditionExpression(results,el.dataset.conditionExpression||"");}function timeFormatHint(format){return format==="12h"?"h:mm AM/PM":"HH:mm";}function parseTimeValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let evaluateSingleCondition(condition){if(!condition||!condition.fieldKey||!condition.operator){return true;}const actual=readFieldValue(condition.fieldKey);const actual===false||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="false"||String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isBlank"){return String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isNotBlank"){return String(actual)!=="";}return true;}function evaluateConditionExpression(results,expression){if(!results.length){return true;}if(results.length===1){return results[0];}const normalized=((expression||"").trim()||results.map(function(_,index){return String(index+1);}).join(" AND ")).replace(/\(/g," ( ").replace(/\)/g," ) ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();const isValidDateParts(year,month,day){const test=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));return test.getUTCFullYear()===year&&test.getUTCMonth()+1===month&&test.getUTCDate()===day;}function parseDateValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);if(match){const year=Number(match[1]);const normalized=raw.replace(/[.\-]/g,"/");match=normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);if(!match){return null;}const first=Number(match[1]);const second=Number(match[2]);const "";}const day=String(parsed.day).padStart(2,"0");const month=String(parsed.month).padStart(2,"0");const year=String(parsed.year).padStart(4,"0");return timeFormatHint(format){return format==="12h"?"h:mm AM/PM":"HH:mm";}function parseTimeValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match;if(format==="12h"){match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*([AaPp][Mm])$/);if(!match){return null;}let hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<1||hours>12||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}const meridiem=match[3].toUpperCase();if(meridiem==="AM"&&hours===12){hours=0;}else if(meridiem==="PM"&&hours<12){hours+=12;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match){return null;}const hours=Number(match[1]);const !!first.checked;}if(first.type==="radio"){const checked=form.querySelector("[name='"+fieldKey+"']:checked");return checked?checked.value:"";}return first.value||"";}function evaluateSingleCondition(condition){if(!condition||!condition.fieldKey||!condition.operator){return true;}const actual=readFieldValue(condition.fieldKey);const compare=condition.value||"";if(condition.operator==="equals"){return String(actual)===compare;}if(condition.operator==="notEquals"){return String(actual)!==compare;}if(condition.operator==="isTrue"){return actual===true||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="true";}if(condition.operator==="isFalse"){return actual===false||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="false"||String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isBlank"){return String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isNotBlank"){return String(actual)!=="";}return true;}function evaluateConditionExpression(results,expression){if(!results.length){return true;}if(results.length===1){return results[0];}const normalized=((expression||"").trim()||results.map(function(_,index){return String(index+1);}).join(" AND ")).replace(/\(/g," ( ").replace(/\)/g," ) ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();const tokens=normalized?normalized.split(" "):[];const values=[];const operators=[];function applyOperator(){if(values.length<2||!operators.length){return;}const operator=operators.pop();const right=values.pop();const left=values.pop();values.push(operator==="AND"?(left&&right):(left||right));}function precedence(operator){return operator==="AND"?2:1;}for(let index=0;index<tokens.length;index+=1){const token=tokens[index];if(!token){continue;}if(token==="("){operators.push(token);continue;}if(token===")"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("){applyOperator();}if(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]==="("){operators.pop();}continue;}if(token==="AND"||token==="OR"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("&&precedence(operators[operators.length-1])>=precedence(token)){applyOperator();}operators.push(token);continue;}if(/^\d+$/.test(token)){const refIndex=Number(token)-1;values.push(refIndex>=0&&refIndex<results.length?results[refIndex]:false);}}while(operators.length){applyOperator();}return values.length?!!values[0]:false;}function isValidDateParts(year,month,day){const test=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));return test.getUTCFullYear()===year&&test.getUTCMonth()+1===month&&test.getUTCDate()===day;}function parseDateValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);if(match){const year=Number(match[1]);const month=Number(match[2]);const day=Number(match[3]);if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:match[1]+"-"+match[2]+"-"+match[3]};}const normalized=raw.replace(/[.\-]/g,"/");match=normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);if(!match){return null;}const first=Number(match[1]);const second=Number(match[2]);const year=Number(match[3]);const month=format==="eu"?second:first;const day=format==="eu"?first:second;if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}const monthText=String(month).padStart(2,"0");const dayText=String(day).padStart(2,"0");return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:String(year).padStart(4,"0")+"-"+monthText+"-"+dayText};}function formatParsedDate(parsed,format){if(!parsed){return "";}const day=String(parsed.day).padStart(2,"0");const month=String(parsed.month).padStart(2,"0");const year=String(parsed.year).padStart(4,"0");return format==="eu"?day+"/"+month+"/"+year:month+"/"+day+"/"+year;}function formatDateForField(value,field){const format=field&&field.dataset&&field.dataset.dateFormat?field.dataset.dateFormat:"us";const parsed=parseDateValue(value,format)||parseDateValue(value,"us")||parseDateValue(value,"eu");return parsed?formatParsedDate(parsed,format):value==null?"":String(value);}function validateDateField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsDate!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.dateFormat||"us";const raw=String(el.value||"").trim();if(!raw){el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}const parsed=parseDateValue(raw,format);if(!parsed){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateInvalid","Please enter a valid date in {format} format."),{format:dateFormatHint(format)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}const minDate=el.dataset.effectiveMinDate||"";const maxDate=el.dataset.effectiveMaxDate||"";if(minDate&&parsed.iso<minDate){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateMin","Please enter a date on or after {date}."),{date:formatDateForField(minDate,el)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}if(maxDate&&parsed.iso>maxDate){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("dateMax","Please enter a date on or before {date}."),{date:formatDateForField(maxDate,el)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}function timeFormatHint(format){return format==="12h"?"h:mm AM/PM":"HH:mm";}function parseTimeValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match;if(format==="12h"){match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*([AaPp][Mm])$/);if(!match){return null;}let hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<1||hours>12||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}const meridiem=match[3].toUpperCase();if(meridiem==="AM"&&hours===12){hours=0;}else if(meridiem==="PM"&&hours<12){hours+=12;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match){return null;}const hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<0||hours>23||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}function formatTimeForField(value,field){const format=field&&field.dataset&&field.dataset.timeFormat?field.dataset.timeFormat:"24h";const parsed=parseTimeValue(value,"24h")||parseTimeValue(value,"12h");if(!parsed){return value==null?"":String(value);}if(format!=="12h"){return parsed;}const parts=parsed.split(":");const hour24=Number(parts[0]);const meridiem=hour24>=12?"PM":"AM";const hour12=hour24%12||12;return String(hour12)+":"+parts[1]+" "+meridiem;}function validateTimeField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsTime!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.timeFormat||"24h";const raw=String(el.value||"").trim();if(!raw){el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}const parsed=parseTimeValue(raw,format);if(!parsed){el.setCustomValidity(interpolate(t("timeInvalid","Please enter a valid time in {format} format."),{format:timeFormatHint(format)}));if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}function readFieldValue(fieldKey){const fields=form.querySelectorAll("[name='"+fieldKey+"']");if(!fields.length){return "";}const first=fields[0];if(first.type==="checkbox"){return !!first.checked;}if(first.type==="radio"){const checked=form.querySelector("[name='"+fieldKey+"']:checked");return checked?checked.value:"";}return first.value||"";}function evaluateSingleCondition(condition){if(!condition||!condition.fieldKey||!condition.operator){return true;}const actual=readFieldValue(condition.fieldKey);const compare=condition.value||"";if(condition.operator==="equals"){return String(actual)===compare;}if(condition.operator==="notEquals"){return String(actual)!==compare;}if(condition.operator==="isTrue"){return actual===true||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="true";}if(condition.operator==="isFalse"){return actual===false||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="false"||String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isBlank"){return String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isNotBlank"){return String(actual)!=="";}return true;}function evaluateConditionExpression(results,expression){if(!results.length){return true;}if(results.length===1){return results[0];}const normalized=((expression||"").trim()||results.map(function(_,index){return String(index+1);}).join(" AND ")).replace(/\(/g," ( ").replace(/\)/g," ) ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();const tokens=normalized?normalized.split(" "):[];const values=[];const operators=[];function applyOperator(){if(values.length<2||!operators.length){return;}const operator=operators.pop();const right=values.pop();const left=values.pop();values.push(operator==="AND"?(left&&right):(left||right));}function precedence(operator){return operator==="AND"?2:1;}for(let index=0;index<tokens.length;index+=1){const token=tokens[index];if(!token){continue;}if(token==="("){operators.push(token);continue;}if(token===")"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("){applyOperator();}if(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]==="("){operators.pop();}continue;}if(token==="AND"||token==="OR"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("&&precedence(operators[operators.length-1])>=precedence(token)){applyOperator();}operators.push(token);continue;}if(/^\d+$/.test(token)){const refIndex=Number(token)-1;values.push(refIndex>=0&&refIndex<results.length?results[refIndex]:false);}}while(operators.length){applyOperator();}return values.length?!!values[0]:false;}function evaluateElementConditions(el){let conditions=[];try{conditions=JSON.parse(el.dataset.conditions||"[]")||[];}catch(error){conditions=[];}if(!conditions.length&&el.dataset.conditionSource){conditions=[{fieldKey:el.dataset.conditionSource,operator:el.dataset.conditionOperator,value:el.dataset.conditionValue||""}];}if(!conditions.length){return true;}const results=conditions.map(function(condition){return evaluateSingleCondition(condition);});return evaluateConditionExpression(results,el.dataset.conditionExpression||"");}function setFieldValue(fieldKey,value){if(formulaFieldKeySet().has(String(fieldKey||""))){return;}const fields=form.querySelectorAll("[name='"+fieldKey+"']");if(!fields.length){return;}fields.forEach(function(el){if(el.type==="checkbox"){el.checked=!!value;}else if(el.dataset.nativeformsTime==="true"){el.value=formatTimeForField(value,el);validateTimeField(el,false);}else{el.value=value==null?"":value;}});}function readAllFieldValues(){const values={};form.querySelectorAll("[name]").forEach(function(control){const fieldKey=String(control.name||"");if(!fieldKey||Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(values,fieldKey)){return;}values[fieldKey]=readRuntimeFieldValue(fieldKey);});return values;}const repeatDeleteState={};let lastPrefillPayload=null;function readRepeatFieldValueByKey(rowEl,fieldKey){const isValidDateParts(year,month,day){const test=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));return test.getUTCFullYear()===year&&test.getUTCMonth()+1===month&&test.getUTCDate()===day;}function parseDateValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);if(match){const year=Number(match[1]);const normalized=raw.replace(/[.\-]/g,"/");match=normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);if(!match){return null;}const first=Number(match[1]);const second=Number(match[2]);const year=Number(match[3]);const month=format==="eu"?second:first;const day=format==="eu"?first:second;if(!isValidDateParts(year,month,day)){return null;}const monthText=String(month).padStart(2,"0");const dayText=String(day).padStart(2,"0");return{year:year,month:month,day:day,iso:String(year).padStart(4,"0")+"-"+monthText+"-"+dayText};}function formatParsedDate(parsed,format){if(!parsed){return "";}const day=String(parsed.day).padStart(2,"0");const month=String(parsed.month).padStart(2,"0");const year=String(parsed.year).padStart(4,"0");return validateDateField(el,showMessage){if(!el||el.dataset.nativeformsDate!=="true"){return true;}const format=el.dataset.dateFormat||"us";const el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}const minDate=el.dataset.effectiveMinDate||"";const el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return false;}el.setCustomValidity("");if(showMessage&&typeof el.reportValidity==="function"){el.reportValidity();}return true;}function timeFormatHint(format){return format==="12h"?"h:mm AM/PM":"HH:mm";}function parseTimeValue(value,format){const raw=String(value||"").trim();if(!raw){return null;}let match;if(format==="12h"){match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*([AaPp][Mm])$/);if(!match){return null;}let hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<1||hours>12||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}const meridiem=match[3].toUpperCase();if(meridiem==="AM"&&hours===12){hours=0;}else if(meridiem==="PM"&&hours<12){hours+=12;}return String(hours).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(minutes).padStart(2,"0");}match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match){return null;}const hours=Number(match[1]);const minutes=Number(match[2]);if(!Number.isInteger(hours)||!Number.isInteger(minutes)||hours<0||hours>23||minutes<0||minutes>59){return null;}return readFieldValue(fieldKey){const fields=form.querySelectorAll("[name='"+fieldKey+"']");if(!fields.length){return "";}const first=fields[0];if(first.type==="checkbox"){return !!first.checked;}if(first.type==="radio"){const checked=form.querySelector("[name='"+fieldKey+"']:checked");return checked?checked.value:"";}return first.value||"";}function evaluateSingleCondition(condition){if(!condition||!condition.fieldKey||!condition.operator){return true;}const actual=readFieldValue(condition.fieldKey);const compare=condition.value||"";if(condition.operator==="equals"){return String(actual)===compare;}if(condition.operator==="notEquals"){return String(actual)!==compare;}if(condition.operator==="isTrue"){return actual===true||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="true";}if(condition.operator==="isFalse"){return actual===false||String(actual).toLowerCase()==="false"||String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isBlank"){return String(actual)==="";}if(condition.operator==="isNotBlank"){return String(actual)!=="";}return true;}function evaluateConditionExpression(results,expression){if(!results.length){return true;}if(results.length===1){return results[0];}const normalized=((expression||"").trim()||results.map(function(_,index){return String(index+1);}).join(" AND ")).replace(/\(/g," ( ").replace(/\)/g," ) ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();const tokens=normalized?normalized.split(" "):[];const values=[];const operators=[];function applyOperator(){if(values.length<2||!operators.length){return;}const operator=operators.pop();const right=values.pop();const left=values.pop();values.push(operator==="AND"?(left&&right):(left||right));}function precedence(operator){return operator==="AND"?2:1;}for(let index=0;index<tokens.length;index+=1){const token=tokens[index];if(!token){continue;}if(token==="("){operators.push(token);continue;}if(token===")"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("){applyOperator();}if(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]==="("){operators.pop();}continue;}if(token==="AND"||token==="OR"){while(operators.length&&operators[operators.length-1]!=="("&&precedence(operators[operators.length-1])>=precedence(token)){applyOperator();}operators.push(token);continue;}if(/^\d+$/.test(token)){const refIndex=Number(token)-1;values.push(refIndex>=0&&refIndex<results.length?results[refIndex]:false);}}while(operators.length){applyOperator();}return values.length?!!values[0]:false;}function evaluateElementConditions(el){let conditions=[];try{conditions=JSON.parse(el.dataset.conditions||"[]")||[];}catch(error){conditions=[];}if(!conditions.length&&el.dataset.conditionSource){conditions=[{fieldKey:el.dataset.conditionSource,operator:el.dataset.conditionOperator,value:el.dataset.conditionValue||""}];}if(!conditions.length){return true;}const results=conditions.map(function(condition){return evaluateSingleCondition(condition);});return evaluateConditionExpression(results,el.dataset.conditionExpression||"");}function           Parameter 
          cache-control
          Evidence 
          no-cache
          Solution

          For secure content, ensure the cache-control HTTP header is set with "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate". If an asset should be cached consider setting the directives "public, max-age, immutable".

Appendix

Alert Types

This section contains additional information on the types of alerts in the report.

  1. Content Security Policy (CSP) Header Not Set

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Content Security Policy (CSP) Header Not Set)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Guides/CSP
    2. https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Content_Security_Policy_Cheat_Sheet.html
    3. https://www.w3.org/TR/CSP/
    4. https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/
    5. https://web.dev/articles/csp
    6. https://caniuse.com/#feat=contentsecuritypolicy
    7. https://content-security-policy.com/

  2. Server Leaks Version Information via "Server" HTTP Response Header Field

    Source raised by a passive scanner (HTTP Server Response Header)
    CWE ID 497
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://httpd.apache.org/docs/current/mod/core.html#servertokens
    2. https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/previous-versions/msp-n-p/ff648552(v=pandp.10)
    3. https://www.troyhunt.com/shhh-dont-let-your-response-headers/

  3. Information Disclosure - Sensitive Information in URL

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Information Disclosure - Sensitive Information in URL)
    CWE ID 598
    WASC ID 13

  4. Information Disclosure - Suspicious Comments

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Information Disclosure - Suspicious Comments)
    CWE ID 615
    WASC ID 13

  5. Modern Web Application

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Modern Web Application)

  6. Re-examine Cache-control Directives

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Re-examine Cache-control Directives)
    CWE ID 525
    WASC ID 13
    Reference
    1. https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Session_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html#web-content-caching
    2. https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Reference/Headers/Cache-Control
    3. https://grayduck.mn/2021/09/13/cache-control-recommendations/
